   1++6E5B              ; ============================================================================
   2++6E5B              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6E5B              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6E5B              ;
   5++6E5B              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6E5B              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6E5B              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6E5B              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6E5B              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6E5B              ; ============================================================================
  11++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6E5B              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6E5B
  18++6E5B              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6E5B                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6E5B 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6E5E 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6E61 B7                   or      a
  23++6E62 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6E64 C2 A3 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6E67
  26++6E67                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6E67 21 AB 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6E6A 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6E6D B7                   or      a
  30++6E6E ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6E70 DA A3 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6E73 CA A3 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6E76 22 BD 6E             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6E79 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6E7A FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6E7C D2 A3 6E             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6E7F
  38++6E7F 21 AB 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6E82 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6E85 B7                   or      a
  41++6E86 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6E88 DA 91 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6E8B CA 91 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6E8E C3 A3 6E             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6E91              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6E91                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6E91 3A 58 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6E94 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6E96 C2 A3 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6E99 3A 55 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6E9C FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6E9E C2 A3 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6EA1 B7                   or      a
  54++6EA2 C9                   ret
  55++6EA3
  56++6EA3              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6EA3 37                   scf
  58++6EA4 C9                   ret
  59++6EA5
  60++6EA5              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6EA5              p127_layout_report:
  62++6EA5 21 BF 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6EA8 CD 06 63             call    p127_puts
  64++6EAB 3A BE 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6EAE CD 22 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6EB1 3A BD 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6EB4 CD 22 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6EB7 21 DB 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6EBA C3 06 63             jp      p127_puts
  70++6EBD
  71++6EBD              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6EBD 00 00                dw      0
  73++6EBF              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6EBF 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6EC3 37 5D 20 4B

 454+ 6EF7                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6EF7              ; ============================================================================
   2++6EF7              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6EF7              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6EF7              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6EF7              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6EF7              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6EF7              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6EF7              ; ============================================================================
   9++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6EF7
  13++6EF7              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6EF7              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6EF7              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6EF7              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6EF7              p127_resident_start:
  18++6EF7
  19++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6EF7              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6EF7
  26++6EF7              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6EF7              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6EF7              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6EF7              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6EF7              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6EF7 32 81 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6EFA 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6EFB 32 82 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6EFE 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6EFF 32 83 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6F02 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6F03 32 84 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6F06
  39++6F06 3A 81 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6F09 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6F0B C2 35 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6F0E 3A 82 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6F11 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6F13 C2 35 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6F16 3A 83 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6F19 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6F1B C2 35 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6F1E 3A 84 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6F21 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6F23 CA 35 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6F26                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6F26 3A 81 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6F29 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6F2B CA 35 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6F2E FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6F30 CA 35 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6F33 B7                   or      a
  58++6F34 C9                   ret
  59++6F35
  60++6F35              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6F35 37                   scf
  62++6F36 C9                   ret
  63++6F37
  64++6F37              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6F37              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6F37              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6F37 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6F39 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6F3B 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6F3D 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6F3F CD F7 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6F42 DA 7F 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6F45
  74++6F45 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6F47 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6F49 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6F4B 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6F4D CD F7 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6F50 D2 7F 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6F53
  81++6F53 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6F55 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6F57 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6F59 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6F5B CD F7 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6F5E D2 7F 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6F61
  88++6F61 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6F63 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6F65 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6F67 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6F69 CD F7 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6F6C D2 7F 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6F6F
  95++6F6F 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6F71 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6F73 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6F75 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6F77 CD F7 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6F7A D2 7F 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6F7D B7                   or      a
 102++6F7E C9                   ret
 103++6F7F
 104++6F7F              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++6F7F 37                   scf
 106++6F80 C9                   ret
 107++6F81
 108++6F81              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++6F81 00                   db      0
 110++6F82              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++6F82 00                   db      0
 112++6F83              p127_resident_arg_stack:
 113++6F83 00                   db      0

 119++6F85              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1

 122++6F85              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0

 125++6F85              p127_w3_init:

 127++6F87 32 22 71             ld      (p127_w3_state),a

 130++6F8F AF                   xor     a
 131++6F90 32 24 71             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++6F93 B7                   or      a
 133++6F94 C9                   ret
 134++6F95
 135++6F95              p127_w3_ui_enter:
 136++6F95 3A 22 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)

 139++6F9C 3A 23 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)

 349++7128              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7128              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7128              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7128              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7128              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7128              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7128              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++7128              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:

 362++7130 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++7132 DA 75 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++7135

 368++713C ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++713E D2 75 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 377++714D

 380++7153 B7                   or      a
 381++7154 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++7156 D2 75 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++7159
 384++7159                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++7159                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.

 387++715C 39                   add     hl,sp

   1++7177              ; ============================================================================
   2++7177              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++7177              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++7177              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++7177              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++7177              ; ============================================================================
   7++7177              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++7177              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++7177              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++7177              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++7177              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++7177              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++7177
  14++7177              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++7177              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++7177              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++7177              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++7177              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++7177              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++7177              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++7177              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++7177              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++7177              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++7177              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++7177
  26++7177              p127_irq_init:
  27++7177 F3                   di
  28++7178 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++717A 32 5A 74             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++717D AF                   xor     a
  31++717E 32 5B 74             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++7181 32 5C 74             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++7184 32 5D 74             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++7187 B7                   or      a
  35++7188 C9                   ret
  36++7189

  38++7189              p127_irq_validate:

  46++719C FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET

  48++71A1 B7                   or      a

 239++72E8              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 240++72E8              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 241++72E8              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and

   1++745E              ; ============================================================================
   2++745E              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++745E              ; Module version: 0.4.4
   4++745E              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++745E              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++745E              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++745E              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++745E              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++745E              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++745E              ; ============================================================================
  11++745E              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++745E              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4

   2++77D3              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++77D3              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++77D3              ; ABI:
   5++77D3              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++77D3              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++77D3              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++77D3              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++77D3              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++77D3              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++77D3              ; ============================================================================

  13++77D3              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

 458+ 78ED                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_arena.asm"
# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++78ED              ; ============================================================================
   2++78ED              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++78ED              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++78ED              ;
   5++78ED              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++78ED              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++78ED              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++78ED              ;
   9++78ED              ; API:
  10++78ED              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++78ED              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++78ED              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++78ED              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++78ED              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++78ED              ;
  16++78ED              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++78ED              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++78ED              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++78ED              ; ============================================================================
  20++78ED              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++78ED              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++78ED              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++78ED              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++78ED              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++78ED
  26++78ED              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++78ED              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++78ED              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++78ED              p127_arena_init:
  30++78ED 21 92 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++78F0 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++78F2 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE

   1++7A4B              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A4B              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7A4B              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A4B              ;
   5++7A4B              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7A4B              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7A4B              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7A4B              ;
   9++7A4B              ; API:
  10++7A4B              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7A4B              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7A4B              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7A4B              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7A4B              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7A4B              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7A4B              ; ============================================================================

  18++7A4B              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

   1++7ECC              ; ============================================================================
   2++7ECC              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7ECC              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7ECC              ;
   5++7ECC              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7ECC              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7ECC              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7ECC              ;
   9++7ECC              ; API inputs:
  10++7ECC              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7ECC              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7ECC              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7ECC              ; Outputs:
  14++7ECC              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7ECC              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7ECC              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7ECC              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7ECC              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7ECC              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7ECC              ; ============================================================================
  21++7ECC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7ECC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7ECC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7ECC              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7ECC              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7ECC
  27++7ECC              p127_as_copy:
  28++7ECC AF                   xor     a
  29++7ECD 32 CB 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
  30++7ED0 32 C7 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),a
  31++7ED3 32 C8 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+1),a
  32++7ED6 32 C9 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),a
  33++7ED9 32 CA 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+3),a
  34++7EDC
  35++7EDC                      ; Validate source and destination completely before taking W3.
  36++7EDC 21 BB 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
  37++7EDF 11 B8 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  38++7EE2 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  39++7EE5 ED B0                ldir
  40++7EE7 21 C3 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length
  41++7EEA 11 BC 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_length
  42++7EED 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  43++7EF0 ED B0                ldir
  44++7EF2 CD EC 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  45++7EF5 DA DE 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  46++7EF8
  47++7EF8 21 BF 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination
  48++7EFB 11 B8 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  49++7EFE 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  50++7F01 ED B0                ldir
  51++7F03 CD EC 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  52++7F06 DA DE 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  53++7F09
  54++7F09                      ; Empty spans are valid no-ops, including an address exactly 4 MiB.
  55++7F09 3A C3 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  56++7F0C 47                   ld      b,a

 177++857A              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++857A              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++857A              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++857A              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++857A              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++857A              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++857A              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++857A              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++857A              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++857A                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++857A                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++857A 5F                   ld      e,a
 189++857B AF                   xor     a

   1++8770              ; ============================================================================
   2++8770              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++8770              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8770              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++8770              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++8770              ; ============================================================================
   7++8770              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++8770              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++8770              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++8770
  11++8770              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++8770              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++8770              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++8770              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++8770              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++8770              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++8770              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++8770
  19++8770              ; IN: none.
  20++8770              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++8770              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++8770              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++8770              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++8770              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++8770              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++8770              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

   2++880B              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader

  60++8850              ; accepts a zero-based sector in E and internally selects physical sector E+1.

 466+ 8D58                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_speaker.asm"
# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8D58              ; ============================================================================
   2++8D58              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8D58              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8D58              ;
   5++8D58              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8D58              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8D58              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8D58              ;
   9++8D58              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8D58              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8D58              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8D58              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8D58              ;
  14++8D58              ; API:
  15++8D58              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8D58              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8D58              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8D58              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8D58              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8D58              ;
  21++8D58              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8D58              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8D58              ; is assumed.
  24++8D58              ; ============================================================================
  25++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8D58
  29++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8D58              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8D58
  33++8D58              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8D58 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8D5A 32 B6 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8D5D C9                   ret
  37++8D5E
  38++8D5E              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8D5E              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8D5E              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8D5E              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8D5E C5                   push    bc
  43++8D5F 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8D60 3A B6 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8D63 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8D65 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8D66 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8D67 E6 01                and     1
  49++8D69 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8D6B 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8D6C F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8D6E 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8D70              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8D70 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8D71              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8D71 32 B6 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8D74 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8D76 C1                   pop     bc
  59++8D77 C9                   ret
  60++8D78
  61++8D78              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8D78              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8D78 3A B6 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8D7B EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8D7D 32 B6 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8D80 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8D82 C9                   ret
  68++8D83
  69++8D83              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8D83              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8D83 3A B6 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8D86 E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8D88 32 B6 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8D8B D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8D8D C9                   ret
  76++8D8E
  77++8D8E              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8D8E 3A B6 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8D91 C9                   ret
  80++8D92
  81++8D92
  82++8D92              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8D92              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8D92              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8D92              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8D92              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8D92 F5                   push    af
  88++8D93 C5                   push    bc
  89++8D94 D5                   push    de
  90++8D95 E5                   push    hl
  91++8D96 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8D98 CD 58 8D             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8D9B 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8D9E              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8D9E CD 78 8D             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8DA1 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8DA3              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8DA3 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8DA5 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8DA7              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8DA7 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8DA9 1B                   dec     de
 103++8DAA 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8DAB B3                   or      e
 105++8DAC 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8DAE CD 83 8D             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8DB1 E1                   pop     hl
 108++8DB2 D1                   pop     de
 109++8DB3 C1                   pop     bc
 110++8DB4 F1                   pop     af
 111++8DB5 C9                   ret
 112++8DB6
 113++8DB6 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0
 114++8DB7

   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.4
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6
  10+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 80
  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12
  13+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE7 EQU #78
  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25
  19+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_FRAME_SIZE EQU 4000
  20+ A000              P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT EQU #00EF
  21+ A000
  22+ A000 06           p126_atm_video_mode: db P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0
  24+ A002 00           Console_X: db 0
  25+ A003 0C           Console_Y: db BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0
  28+ A006 00           bios_dbg_char: db 0
  29+ A007 00           bios_dbg_write_row: db 0
  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0
  31+ A009 00           bios_dbg_parity: db 0
  32+ A00A 00           bios_dbg_attr: db 0
  33+ A00B 00 00        bios_dbg_offset: dw 0
  34+ A00D 00 00        bios_dbg_text_ptr: dw 0
  35+ A00F 00           bios_dbg_print_row: db 0
  36+ A010 00           bios_dbg_print_col: db 0
  37+ A011
  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret
 150+ A09B
 151+ A09B
 152+ A09B              p127_ui_init:
 153+ A09B CD 11 A0             call p126_atm_video_init
 154+ A09E CD A8 A0             call BIOS_DebugConsole_Init
 155+ A0A1 CD B8 A0             call BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
 156+ A0A4 CD 3E A1             call BIOS_DebugConsole_DrawInterface
 157+ A0A7 C9                   ret
 158+ A0A8
 159+ A0A8              BIOS_DebugConsole_Init:
 159+ A0A8
 160+ A0A8 AF                   xor     a
 161+ A0A9 32 04 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
 162+ A0AC 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 163+ A0AF 32 02 A0             ld      (Console_X), a
 164+ A0B2 3E 0C                ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 165+ A0B4 32 03 A0             ld      (Console_Y), a
 166+ A0B7 C9                   ret
 167+ A0B8
 168+ A0B8              BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear:
 168+ A0B8
 169+ A0B8 F5                   push    af
 170+ A0B9 C5                   push    bc
 171+ A0BA D5                   push    de
 172+ A0BB E5                   push    hl
 173+ A0BC CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 174+ A0BF
 175+ A0BF                      ; Clear 25 rows x 64-byte stride in both character parity planes.
 176+ A0BF 21 C0 41             ld      hl, #41C0
 177+ A0C2 11 C1 41             ld      de, #41C1
 178+ A0C5 36 20                ld      (hl), 32
 179+ A0C7 01 3F 06             ld      bc, #063F
 180+ A0CA ED B0                ldir
 181+ A0CC 21 C0 61             ld      hl, #61C0
 182+ A0CF 11 C1 61             ld      de, #61C1
 183+ A0D2 36 20                ld      (hl), 32
 184+ A0D4 01 3F 06             ld      bc, #063F
 185+ A0D7 ED B0                ldir
 186+ A0D9
 187+ A0D9                      ; Default attributes, then color the status panel blue/white.
 188+ A0D9 21 C0 E1             ld      hl, #E1C0
 189+ A0DC 11 C1 E1             ld      de, #E1C1
 190+ A0DF 36 47                ld      (hl), #47
 191+ A0E1 01 3F 06             ld      bc, #063F
 192+ A0E4 ED B0                ldir
 193+ A0E6 21 C1 C1             ld      hl, #C1C1
 194+ A0E9 11 C2 C1             ld      de, #C1C2
 195+ A0EC 36 47                ld      (hl), #47
 196+ A0EE 01 3F 06             ld      bc, #063F
 197+ A0F1 ED B0                ldir
 198+ A0F3
 199+ A0F3 21 C0 E1             ld      hl, #E1C0
 200+ A0F6 11 C1 E1             ld      de, #E1C1
 201+ A0F9 36 4F                ld      (hl), #4F
 202+ A0FB 01 FF 02             ld      bc, #02FF
 203+ A0FE ED B0                ldir
 204+ A100 21 C1 C1             ld      hl, #C1C1
 205+ A103 11 C2 C1             ld      de, #C1C2
 206+ A106 36 4F                ld      (hl), #4F
 207+ A108 01 FF 02             ld      bc, #02FF
 208+ A10B ED B0                ldir
 209+ A10D
 210+ A10D                      ; Lower log area: black background, green foreground.
 211+ A10D 21 C0 E4             ld      hl, #E4C0
 212+ A110 11 C1 E4             ld      de, #E4C1
 213+ A113 36 44                ld      (hl), #44
 214+ A115 01 3F 03             ld      bc, #033F
 215+ A118 ED B0                ldir
 216+ A11A 21 C1 C4             ld      hl, #C4C1
 217+ A11D 11 C2 C4             ld      de, #C4C2
 218+ A120 36 44                ld      (hl), #44
 219+ A122 01 3F 03             ld      bc, #033F
 220+ A125 ED B0                ldir
 221+ A127
 222+ A127 CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 223+ A12A AF                   xor     a
 224+ A12B 32 04 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
 225+ A12E 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 226+ A131 32 02 A0             ld      (Console_X), a
 227+ A134 3E 0C                ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 228+ A136 32 03 A0             ld      (Console_Y), a
 229+ A139 E1                   pop     hl
 230+ A13A D1                   pop     de
 231+ A13B C1                   pop     bc
 232+ A13C F1                   pop     af
 233+ A13D C9                   ret
 234+ A13E
 235+ A13E              BIOS_DebugConsole_DrawInterface:
 235+ A13E
 236+ A13E F5                   push    af
 237+ A13F C5                   push    bc
 238+ A140 E5                   push    hl
 239+ A141
 240+ A141 21 9A A3             ld      hl, p126_ui_line0
 241+ A144 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 242+ A146 0E 01                ld      c, 1
 243+ A148 CD 63 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt
 244+ A14B
 245+ A14B 21 C2 A3             ld      hl, p126_ui_line1
 246+ A14E 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 247+ A150 0E 01                ld      c, 1
 248+ A152 CD 63 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt
 249+ A155
 250+ A155 21 E8 A3             ld      hl, p126_ui_line11
 251+ A158              .p126_draw_rtc_line:
 252+ A158 3E 0B                ld      a, 11
 253+ A15A 0E 01                ld      c, 1
 254+ A15C CD 63 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt
 255+ A15F
 256+ A15F E1                   pop     hl
 257+ A160 C1                   pop     bc
 258+ A161 F1                   pop     af
 259+ A162 C9                   ret
 260+ A163
 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 263+ A163
 264+ A163 F5                   push    af
 265+ A164 C5                   push    bc
 266+ A165 D5                   push    de
 267+ A166 E5                   push    hl
 268+ A167 CD 95 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 313+ A1BA 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:
 316+ A1C0 CD B0 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 318+ A1C6 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 319+ A1C8 ED 79                out     (c),a
 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af
 326+ A1D2 C9                   ret
 327+ A1D3              .p126_print_at_denied:
 328+ A1D3 E1                   pop     hl
 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de
 330+ A1D5 C1                   pop     bc
 331+ A1D6 F1                   pop     af
 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret
 333+ A1D8
 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.
 335+ A1D8              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar:
 335+ A1D8
 336+ A1D8 F5                   push    af
 337+ A1D9 CD 95 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 338+ A1DC DA 75 A2             jp      c,.p126_console_denied
 339+ A1DF F1                   pop     af
 340+ A1E0 F5                   push    af
 341+ A1E1 C5                   push    bc
 342+ A1E2 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 343+ A1E5 ED 79                out     (c),a
 344+ A1E7 C1                   pop     bc
 345+ A1E8 C5                   push    bc
 346+ A1E9 D5                   push    de
 347+ A1EA E5                   push    hl
 348+ A1EB FE 0D                cp      13
 349+ A1ED CA 40 A2             jp      z, .p126_console_cr
 350+ A1F0 FE 0A                cp      10
 351+ A1F2 CA 4A A2             jp      z, .p126_console_lf
 352+ A1F5 FE 08                cp      8
 353+ A1F7 CA 50 A2             jp      z, .p126_console_bs
 354+ A1FA FE 20                cp      32
 355+ A1FC DA 6D A2             jp      c, .p126_console_done
 356+ A1FF FE 80                cp      128
 357+ A201 DA 06 A2             jp      c, .p126_console_char_ok
 358+ A204 3E 3F                ld      a, '?'
 359+ A206              .p126_console_char_ok:
 360+ A206 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 361+ A209 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)
 362+ A20C C6 0C                add     a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 363+ A20E 57                   ld      d, a
 364+ A20F 3A 05 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
 365+ A212 5F                   ld      e, a
 366+ A213 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 367+ A216 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 368+ A219 3A 05 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
 369+ A21C 3C                   inc     a
 370+ A21D FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 371+ A21F DA 2C A2             jp      c, .p126_console_store_col
 372+ A222 AF                   xor     a
 373+ A223 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 374+ A226 CD 15 A3             call    bios_dbg_newline
 375+ A229 C3 2F A2             jp      .p126_console_sync_cursor
 376+ A22C              .p126_console_store_col:
 377+ A22C 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 378+ A22F              .p126_console_sync_cursor:
 379+ A22F 3A 05 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
 380+ A232 32 02 A0             ld      (Console_X), a
 381+ A235 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)
 382+ A238 C6 0C                add     a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 383+ A23A 32 03 A0             ld      (Console_Y), a
 384+ A23D C3 6D A2             jp      .p126_console_done
 385+ A240              .p126_console_cr:
 386+ A240 AF                   xor     a
 387+ A241 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 388+ A244 32 02 A0             ld      (Console_X), a
 389+ A247 C3 6D A2             jp      .p126_console_done
 390+ A24A              .p126_console_lf:
 391+ A24A CD 15 A3             call    bios_dbg_newline
 392+ A24D C3 2F A2             jp      .p126_console_sync_cursor
 393+ A250              .p126_console_bs:
 394+ A250 3A 05 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
 395+ A253 B7                   or      a
 396+ A254 CA 6D A2             jp      z, .p126_console_done
 397+ A257 3D                   dec     a
 398+ A258 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 399+ A25B 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)
 400+ A25E C6 0C                add     a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 401+ A260 57                   ld      d, a
 402+ A261 3A 05 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
 403+ A264 5F                   ld      e, a
 404+ A265 3E 20                ld      a, 32
 405+ A267 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 406+ A26A C3 2F A2             jp      .p126_console_sync_cursor
 407+ A26D              .p126_console_done:
 408+ A26D CD B0 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 409+ A270 E1                   pop     hl
 410+ A271 D1                   pop     de
 411+ A272 C1                   pop     bc
 412+ A273 F1                   pop     af
 413+ A274 C9                   ret
 414+ A275              .p126_console_denied:
 415+ A275 F1                   pop     af
 416+ A276 C9                   ret
 417+ A277
 418+ A277
 419+ A277              p126_screen_write_char:
 420+ A277 F5                   push    af
 421+ A278 C5                   push    bc
 422+ A279 D5                   push    de
 423+ A27A E5                   push    hl
 424+ A27B 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 425+ A27E 7A                   ld      a, d
 426+ A27F 32 07 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_write_row), a
 427+ A282 7B                   ld      a, e
 428+ A283 32 08 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_write_col), a
 429+ A286
 430+ A286 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 431+ A289
 432+ A289                      ; Offset = #01C0 + row*64 + column/2.
 433+ A289 3A 07 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_write_row)
 434+ A28C 6F                   ld      l, a
 435+ A28D 26 00                ld      h, 0
 436+ A28F 29                   add     hl, hl
 437+ A290 29                   add     hl, hl
 438+ A291 29                   add     hl, hl
 439+ A292 29                   add     hl, hl
 440+ A293 29                   add     hl, hl
 441+ A294 29                   add     hl, hl
 442+ A295 11 C0 01             ld      de, #01C0
 443+ A298 19                   add     hl, de
 444+ A299 3A 08 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_write_col)
 445+ A29C 4F                   ld      c, a
 446+ A29D E6 01                and     1
 447+ A29F 32 09 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_parity), a
 448+ A2A2 79                   ld      a, c
 449+ A2A3 CB 3F                srl     a
 450+ A2A5 5F                   ld      e, a
 451+ A2A6 16 00                ld      d, 0
 452+ A2A8 19                   add     hl, de
 453+ A2A9 22 0B A0             ld      (bios_dbg_offset), hl
 454+ A2AC
 455+ A2AC                      ; Header rows use blue/white; the lower log uses green/black.
 456+ A2AC 3A 07 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_write_row)
 457+ A2AF FE 0C                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 458+ A2B1 3E 44                ld      a, #44
 459+ A2B3 D2 B8 A2             jp      nc, .p126_attr_ready
 460+ A2B6 3E 4F                ld      a, #4F
 461+ A2B8              .p126_attr_ready:
 462+ A2B8 32 0A A0             ld      (bios_dbg_attr), a
 463+ A2BB
 464+ A2BB 2A 0B A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_offset)
 465+ A2BE 3A 09 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_parity)
 466+ A2C1 B7                   or      a
 467+ A2C2 C2 DB A2             jp      nz, .p126_write_odd
 468+ A2C5
 469+ A2C5 11 00 40             ld      de, #4000
 470+ A2C8 19                   add     hl, de
 471+ A2C9 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 472+ A2CC 77                   ld      (hl), a
 473+ A2CD 2A 0B A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_offset)
 474+ A2D0 11 00 E0             ld      de, #E000
 475+ A2D3 19                   add     hl, de
 476+ A2D4 3A 0A A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_attr)
 477+ A2D7 77                   ld      (hl), a
 478+ A2D8 C3 EF A2             jp      .p126_write_done
 479+ A2DB
 480+ A2DB              .p126_write_odd:
 481+ A2DB 11 00 60             ld      de, #6000
 482+ A2DE 19                   add     hl, de
 483+ A2DF 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 484+ A2E2 77                   ld      (hl), a

 486+ A2E6 11 00 C0             ld      de, #C000
 487+ A2E9 19                   add     hl, de

 489+ A2EB 3A 0A A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_attr)
 490+ A2EE 77                   ld      (hl), a
 491+ A2EF

 493+ A2EF CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages

 495+ A2F3 D1                   pop     de
 496+ A2F4 C1                   pop     bc
 497+ A2F5 F1                   pop     af
 498+ A2F6 C9                   ret
 499+ A2F7
 500+ A2F7              ; IN: A=character, B=ATM-native attribute, D=row, E=column.
 501+ A2F7
 502+ A2F7              p126_screen_map_video_pages:
 503+ A2F7 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
 504+ A2F9 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 505+ A2FC ED 79                out     (c), a
 506+ A2FE 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
 507+ A300 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 508+ A303 ED 79                out     (c), a
 509+ A305 C9                   ret
 510+ A306
 511+ A306              p126_screen_restore_host_pages:
 512+ A306 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 513+ A308 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 514+ A30B ED 79                out     (c), a
 515+ A30D 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 516+ A30F 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 517+ A312 ED 79                out     (c), a
 518+ A314 C9                   ret

 520+ A315
 521+ A315              ; Advance one row in the 13-row log; scroll rows 13..24 at the bottom.
 522+ A315              bios_dbg_newline:

 524+ A318 3C                   inc     a
 525+ A319 FE 0D                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
 526+ A31B DA 23 A3             jp      c, .p126_newline_store

 531+ A326 AF                   xor     a
 532+ A327 32 05 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 533+ A32A C9                   ret

 535+ A32B              ; Move log rows 13..24 up by one. Each hardware text row has a 64-byte
 536+ A32B              ; stride; the 40 visible bytes are copied together with the row padding.
 537+ A32B              P126_ScrollConsoleUp:

 543+ A332
 544+ A332                      ; Even character plane: page 7, window #4000.
 545+ A332 21 00 45             ld      hl, #4500
 546+ A335 11 C0 44             ld      de, #44C0
 547+ A338 01 00 03             ld      bc, #0300
 548+ A33B ED B0                ldir

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 644+ A43D D8                   ret     c
 645+ A43E 3A C3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_even)
 646+ A441 21 C7 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_even
 647+ A444 BE                   cp      (hl)
 648+ A445 20 2D                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 649+ A447 3A C4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_odd)
 650+ A44A 21 C8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_odd
 651+ A44D BE                   cp      (hl)
 652+ A44E 20 24                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 653+ A450 3A C5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_even)
 654+ A453 21 C9 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_even
 655+ A456 BE                   cp      (hl)
 656+ A457 20 1B                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 657+ A459 3A C6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_odd)
 658+ A45C 21 CA A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd
 659+ A45F BE                   cp      (hl)
 660+ A460 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 661+ A462                      ; Compare complete-page CRCs: P7 character plane and P3 attributes.
 662+ A462 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 663+ A465 11 DC A5             ld      de,p127_video_crc_after
 664+ A468 06 04                ld      b,4
 665+ A46A              p127_video_crc_compare_loop:
 666+ A46A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 667+ A46B BE                   cp      (hl)
 668+ A46C 20 06                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 669+ A46E 23                   inc     hl
 670+ A46F 13                   inc     de
 671+ A470 10 F8                djnz    p127_video_crc_compare_loop
 672+ A472 B7                   or      a
 673+ A473 C9                   ret
 674+ A474
 675+ A474              p127_video_test_fail:
 676+ A474 37                   scf
 677+ A475 C9                   ret
 678+ A476
 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.
 680+ A476              p127_video_switch_mode:
 681+ A476 32 C1 A5             ld      (p127_video_mode_arg),a
 682+ A479 C5                   push    bc
 683+ A47A D5                   push    de
 684+ A47B E5                   push    hl
 685+ A47C CD 95 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 686+ A47F 38 6A                jr      c,p127_video_switch_refused
 687+ A481 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 688+ A484
 689+ A484                      ; Preserve the original P3 bytes occupied by the temporary trampoline.
 690+ A484                      ; The trampoline returns to resident code, where these bytes are restored
 691+ A484                      ; before P3 is unmapped. This keeps the full-page CRC genuinely read-only.
 692+ A484 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 693+ A487 11 CB A5             ld      de,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 694+ A48A 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 695+ A48D ED B0                ldir
 696+ A48F
 697+ A48F                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p127_video_mode_return.
 698+ A48F 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 699+ A492 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 700+ A494 23                   inc     hl
 701+ A495 36 77                ld      (hl),#77
 702+ A497 23                   inc     hl
 703+ A498 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 704+ A49A 23                   inc     hl
 705+ A49B 36 ED                ld      (hl),#ED
 706+ A49D 23                   inc     hl
 707+ A49E 36 79                ld      (hl),#79
 708+ A4A0 23                   inc     hl
 709+ A4A1 36 C3                ld      (hl),#C3
 710+ A4A3 23                   inc     hl
 711+ A4A4 36 AF                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return & #FF
 712+ A4A6 23                   inc     hl
 713+ A4A7 36 A4                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return >> 8
 714+ A4A9 3A C1 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_mode_arg)
 715+ A4AC C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 716+ A4AF
 717+ A4AF              p127_video_mode_return:
 718+ A4AF                      ; P3 is still mapped here: restore the bytes overwritten by the trampoline.
 719+ A4AF 21 CB A5             ld      hl,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 720+ A4B2 11 EC FF             ld      de,#FFEC
 721+ A4B5 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 722+ A4B8 ED B0                ldir
 723+ A4BA CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 724+ A4BD CD B0 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 725+ A4C0
 726+ A4C0                      ; Verify the CPU windows and W3 ownership contract after EVERY mode
 727+ A4C0                      ; write. W2 must be the host page P5, W3 must be P0, and the UI lease
 728+ A4C0                      ; must be released before another mode transition is attempted.
 729+ A4C0 3A 45 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+1)
 730+ A4C3 FE 05                cp      5
 731+ A4C5 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 732+ A4C7 3A 47 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 733+ A4CA B7                   or      a
 734+ A4CB 20 19                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 735+ A4CD 3A 22 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 736+ A4D0 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 737+ A4D2 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 738+ A4D4 3A 23 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 739+ A4D7 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 740+ A4D9 20 0B                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 741+ A4DB 3A 24 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 742+ A4DE B7                   or      a
 743+ A4DF 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 744+ A4E1 E1                   pop     hl
 745+ A4E2 D1                   pop     de
 746+ A4E3 C1                   pop     bc
 747+ A4E4 B7                   or      a
 748+ A4E5 C9                   ret
 749+ A4E6
 750+ A4E6              p127_video_mode_restore_error:
 751+ A4E6 E1                   pop     hl
 752+ A4E7 D1                   pop     de
 753+ A4E8 C1                   pop     bc
 754+ A4E9 37                   scf
 755+ A4EA C9                   ret
 756+ A4EB
 757+ A4EB              p127_video_switch_refused:
 758+ A4EB E1                   pop     hl
 759+ A4EC D1                   pop     de
 760+ A4ED C1                   pop     bc
 761+ A4EE 37                   scf
 762+ A4EF C9                   ret
 763+ A4F0
 764+ A4F0              ; Capture bytes from both character parity planes and both attribute planes.
 765+ A4F0              ; OUT: before signature when p127_video_capture_target=0, after when =1.
 766+ A4F0              p127_video_capture_signature:
 767+ A4F0 C5                   push    bc
 768+ A4F1 D5                   push    de
 769+ A4F2 E5                   push    hl
 770+ A4F3 CD 95 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter

 774+ A4FE B7                   or      a
 775+ A4FF 20 1A                jr      nz,p127_video_capture_after
 776+ A501 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)
 777+ A504 32 C3 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_char_even),a
 778+ A507 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)
 779+ A50A 32 C4 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_char_odd),a
 780+ A50D 3A C0 E1             ld      a,(#E1C0)
 781+ A510 32 C5 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_attr_even),a

 783+ A516 32 C6 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_attr_odd),a
 784+ A519 18 18                jr      p127_video_capture_done
 785+ A51B              p127_video_capture_after:
 786+ A51B 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)
 787+ A51E 32 C7 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_char_even),a
 788+ A521 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)
 789+ A524 32 C8 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_char_odd),a
 790+ A527 3A C0 E1             ld      a,(#E1C0)
 791+ A52A 32 C9 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_attr_even),a

 793+ A530 32 CA A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd),a
 794+ A533              p127_video_capture_done:
 795+ A533                      ; Full-page CRC-16/CCITT over the complete 16 KiB P7 and P3 windows.
 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.
 797+ A533 CD 67 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_pages
 798+ A536 3A C2 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_capture_phase)
 799+ A539 B7                   or      a
 800+ A53A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_crc_store_after
 801+ A53C 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 802+ A53F 18 03                jr      p127_video_crc_store
 803+ A541              p127_video_crc_store_after:
 804+ A541 21 DC A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_after
 805+ A544              p127_video_crc_store:
 806+ A544 3A D4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7)
 807+ A547 77                   ld      (hl),a
 808+ A548 23                   inc     hl
 809+ A549 3A D5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7+1)
 810+ A54C 77                   ld      (hl),a
 811+ A54D 23                   inc     hl
 812+ A54E 3A D6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3)
 813+ A551 77                   ld      (hl),a
 814+ A552 23                   inc     hl
 815+ A553 3A D7 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3+1)
 816+ A556 77                   ld      (hl),a
 817+ A557 CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 818+ A55A CD B0 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 819+ A55D E1                   pop     hl
 820+ A55E D1                   pop     de
 821+ A55F C1                   pop     bc
 822+ A560 B7                   or      a
 823+ A561 C9                   ret
 824+ A562              p127_video_capture_refused:
 825+ A562 E1                   pop     hl
 826+ A563 D1                   pop     de
 827+ A564 C1                   pop     bc
 828+ A565 37                   scf
 829+ A566 C9                   ret
 830+ A567
 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.
 834+ A567              p127_video_crc16_pages:
 835+ A567 C5                   push    bc
 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl
 838+ A56A 21 00 40             ld      hl,#4000
 839+ A56D CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 840+ A570 ED 53 D4 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p7),de
 841+ A574 21 00 C0             ld      hl,#C000
 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 843+ A57A ED 53 D6 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p3),de
 844+ A57E E1                   pop     hl
 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de
 846+ A580 C1                   pop     bc
 847+ A581 C9                   ret
 848+ A582
 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:
 851+ A582 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:
 860+ A590 CB 23                sla     e
 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.

 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.

 575+ AA94
 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ AA94              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ AA94              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ AA94 21 53 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ AA97 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ AA99 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ AA9B              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ AA9B 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ AA9C FE 06                cp      6
 585+ AA9E 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ AAA0 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ AAA2 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ AAA4 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ AAA6 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ AAA8 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ AAAA 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ AAAC FE 02                cp      2
 593+ AAAE 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ AAB0 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ AAB2 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ AAB4 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ AAB6 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ AAB8 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ AABA 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ AABC 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ AABE              p127_physmem_reserved_check:

 610+ AACA E1                   pop     hl
 611+ AACB C1                   pop     bc
 612+ AACC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ AACE              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:

 616+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 617+ AAD2 C9                   ret
 618+ AAD3              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ AAD3 37                   scf
 620+ AAD4 C9                   ret
 621+ AAD5
 622+ AAD5              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ AAD5              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ AAD5 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)

 647+ AAF4
 648+ AAF4              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AAF4              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AAF4              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AAF4 21 53 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AAF7 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AAF9 0E 00                ld      c,0

 660+ AB04 FE 02                cp      2

 662+ AB08 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AB0A 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AB0C FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AB0E 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AB10 FE 05                cp      5

 668+ AB14 FE 07                cp      7

 673+ AB1C 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AB1E              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
